# Smite 2 gods: ability numbers, forms and counterplay (supplement to smite-gods.md)

Research date: 19 November 2026. This file adds what [smite-gods.md](smite-gods.md) left out: for all 96 Smite 2 gods, every ability's damage per rank and its Strength/Intelligence scaling, the mana cost per rank, the cooldown and the other listed numbers, the ability's form (projectile, ground circle, dash, channel and so on), and a one-line counterplay. It does not repeat the Smite 1 gods.

**Source and method.** All numbers come from the Smite 2 wiki's god pages (wiki.smite2.com), read as raw page text through its MediaWiki API on 19 Nov 2026 and parsed by a script. The wiki's `{{Ability}}` boxes carry the values as listed stats (for example `Damage: 30 | 45 | 60 | 75 | 90`, `Cost: 60 | 65 | ... mana`), so the numbers are copied, not re-read by eye. 32 of the 96 pages were edited in November 2026 (latest 17 Nov 2026), the rest in October 2026. Second sources (Mobalytics and similar) were not fetched except one Smitefire page, so **every number is single-source** apart from Ishtar's Rolling Thunder; the spot check against the patch notes below is the other cross-check.

**How to read the per-god entries.**
- `a/b/c/d/e` is rank 1 to rank 5. A single value is the same at every rank. `STR` = Strength, `INT` = Intelligence, `s` = seconds, `m` = metres, `deg` = degrees. Smite 2 ranges are in metres (1 Smite 1 unit = 0.16 m, see smite-gods.md).
- The italic part after the name is the ability's **form**, worked out by script from the wiki's stats and description: *projectile* (the wiki's notes say it stops on a unit), *piercing line* (passes through enemies), *ground circle* (Range plus Radius, placed at a distance), *circle around the god* (Radius only), *cone*, *dash*, *leap*, *teleport*, *channel*, *stealth*, *placed object/summon*, *self buff / empowered attack*. *delay x s* appears only where the wiki gives a delay number.
- **Mechanics** paraphrases the wiki's notes: what the shot stops on (minions, gods, walls), how many hits over how long, whether the caster is CC immune or displacement immune during it, whether it can be reactivated, and which enemy CC it applies.
- **Counter** lines are **inference, not stated by the wiki**. Lines marked *Counter* were written by hand from the ability's wiki description, stop rule and stats; a script then checked each one for claims the wiki text does not support (cover that the stop rule rules out, a CC the ability does not apply, words such as "untargetable" or "destroy"), and the lines it caught were corrected or softened; lines marked *Counter (rule-based)* were produced by script from the stop rule, the shape and the CC (for example "stops on the first unit" leads to "keep a minion between you"). Read the rule-based ones as a first draft. Passives without a hand-written line just say there is nothing to dodge.
- **Data flag** marks a value on the wiki that looks wrong (a rank series that goes down then up, a "Radius" that is really an angle, Range and Radius that look swapped).
- The *Aspect version* line lists the numbers from the page's "God Aspect" section (the optional talent that changes the kit) for each ability the aspect changes.

## Which facts in smite-gods.md need correcting?

### Takeaway
The earlier file's numbers hold up: a script checked every cooldown and every "Dmg a–b" range in it against the wiki and found only two small cooldown slips. The "misread" it reported for Ishtar is not a reading error: the wiki itself labels Rolling Thunder's 2.4 m as Range and 8.8 m as Radius, and the same label swap shows up on three more abilities. Several wiki values themselves look like typos.

### Cited Findings
- **Ishtar, Rolling Thunder: not a misread.** smite-gods.md (Gaps) says its automated reader swapped the range and radius. The raw wiki text says "Range: 2.4 meters" and "Radius: 8.8 meters" ([S2 wiki API, Ishtar](https://wiki.smite2.com/api.php?action=query&prop=revisions&rvprop=content&rvslots=main&format=json&titles=Ishtar)). The description says she calls "four lightning strikes in a line" that she aims, so 8.8 m is most likely the length of the line and 2.4 m the size of each strike; the labels on the wiki appear swapped. A second source agrees on the swap: Smitefire's Smite 2 section for Ishtar (marked Open Beta 48) lists Range 8.8 m and Radius 2.4 m, the same 0.75 s mesmerize, 13.5 s cooldown and 55–75 cost. **But it gives damage 75 / 100 / 125 / 150 / 175 (40% Strength), where the wiki gives 60 / 85 / 110 / 135 / 160** ([Smitefire, Ishtar](https://www.smitefire.com/smite/god/ishtar-122?page=114)). The wiki's 60–160 is the same as the Smite 1 value Smitefire shows, so the wiki may still carry an old number; not settled.
- **The same Range/Radius pattern on other abilities** (Radius larger than Range, no cone): Nut's Warp (Range 2.4 m, Radius 7.2 m) ([S2 wiki, Nut](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Nut)), Ne Zha's Universe Ring Toss (Range 0.96 m, Radius 8.8 m; 0.96 m is a typical projectile radius, so read it as range 8.8 m) ([S2 wiki, Ne Zha](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Ne_Zha)), and Thor's passive Warrior's Madness (Range 3.2 m, Radius 8.8 m) ([S2 wiki, Thor](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Thor)). Treat these as label swaps on the wiki; unconfirmed.
- **"Radius" values that are angles.** The wiki lists "Radius: 120" for Artio's Energy Surge and Maul Prey and for Da Ji's and Gilgamesh's basic attacks, and "Radius: 180" for Thor's Berserker Barrage. These are cone angles in degrees, not metres ([S2 wiki, Artio](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Artio); [Da Ji](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Da_Ji); [Gilgamesh](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Gilgamesh); [Thor](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Thor)).
- **Ravana, Overhead Kick:** smite-gods.md says "CD 14–16 s". The wiki gives 16 / 15.5 / 15 / 14.5 / 14 s, i.e. it falls from 16 to 14 with rank ([S2 wiki, Ravana](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Ravana)).
- **Ullr, axe stance:** smite-gods.md says "All CD 14–15 s". The wiki gives Thrown Axe, Invigorate and Glory Bound a flat 14 s each; 15 s belongs to the bow stance's Hail of Arrows ([S2 wiki, Ullr](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Ullr)).
- **Hun Batz's passive:** smite-gods.md says Infused Strikes "moved to his aspect". The base kit's passive is still named Infused Strikes (+4 Strength per stack, +0.2 per level, 4 stacks, 4 s); the aspect has its own Infused Strikes that slows 10% for 2 s ([S2 wiki, Hun Batz](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Hun_Batz)).
- **Unnamed passives filled in:** Ishtar's passive is Mark of Vengeance, Hou Yi's is Suntouched, Hun Batz's is Infused Strikes ([S2 wiki, Ishtar](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Ishtar); [Hou Yi](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Hou_Yi); [Hun Batz](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Hun_Batz)).
- **Aspect names that smite-gods.md lacked:** Osiris: Aspect of the Fragmented; Pele: Aspect of Obsidian; Poseidon: Aspect of the Trident ([S2 wiki, Osiris](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Osiris); [Pele](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Pele); [Poseidon](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Poseidon)).
- **The Morrigan's passive, Doomsayer:** smite-gods.md gives a 1.6 m explosion. The wiki's stats for it list only 9% of the enemy's health as magical damage and -80% against jungle bosses and Titans, with no radius ([S2 wiki, The Morrigan](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/The_Morrigan)). The 1.6 m figure is unconfirmed.
- **Wiki values that look like typos** (reported as on the wiki; the ones whose rank series goes down and up again are also flagged in the god entries):
  - Aladdin, Agile Run: Genie damage 35 / 50 / **5** / 80 / 95 (65 expected) ([S2 wiki, Aladdin](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Aladdin)).
  - Aladdin, Into The Lamp: cost 70 / 80 / 90 / **00** / 110 (100 expected) (same page).
  - Athena, Confound: cost 60 / 65 / **79** / 75 / 80 (70 expected) ([S2 wiki, Athena](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Athena)).
  - Nut, Warp: cost **50** / 45 / 50 / 55 / 60 (40 expected) ([S2 wiki, Nut](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Nut)).
  - Anhur, Disperse: cooldown 15 / **14.4** / 14 / 13.5 / 13 s (14.5 expected) ([S2 wiki, Anhur](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Anhur)).
  - Uneven but possibly real: Hel's Decay damage 90 / 150 / 190 / 240 / 290 ([S2 wiki, Hel](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Hel)); Awilix's Summon Suku 85 / 135 / 180 / 235 / 285 ([S2 wiki, Awilix](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Awilix)); Pele's Eruption outer rings 0 / 0 / 140 / 180 / 220 (the rings seem to unlock at rank 3) ([S2 wiki, Pele](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Pele)).
- **What was checked and agrees.** Every "CD x s" in smite-gods.md's Smite 2 entries was compared by script with the wiki's cooldown (rank 1 and rank 5): only the Ravana and Ullr lines above differ. All five "Dmg a–b" ranges in it (rank 1 and rank 5) match the wiki. Every other number with a unit in its ability lines (s, m, %) was searched for in the matching wiki ability; apart from the items above, the only misses were derived figures (Hou Yi's Sunbreaker "9 suns over 4.5 s" is 9 strikes at 0.5 s each, as the wiki describes) ([S2 wiki, Hou Yi](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Hou_Yi)).

### Inferences
- The wiki's stat boxes are filled by hand, so a label (Range vs Radius) or a single rank value can be wrong while the rest is right. For tuning a game from these numbers, use the rank series' pattern rather than one odd value.

### Gaps
- Only one second-source check was made (Smitefire, for Ishtar). It confirmed the Range/Radius swap and found a damage disagreement, which suggests other wiki values may lag a patch too. The other label swaps (Nut, Ne Zha, Thor) and the typos were not checked against a second source.
- The checks cover numbers only. Wording claims in smite-gods.md (what an ability does) were not re-verified line by line.

## For each Smite 2 god, what does each ability deal and cost per rank, what form is it, and how is it countered?

### Takeaway
All 96 gods are listed below in full: passive, 1, 2, 3 and ultimate (plus stance and alternate versions), with damage per rank, scaling, cost per rank, cooldown, ranges, CC durations and the aspect's numbers. Typical values, by script over the base kits' 1, 2 and 3 abilities that list a five-rank "Damage" stat (200 abilities; some of those are per-tick values): median damage 75 at rank 1 (middle half 51–85) and 240 at rank 5 (161–290), with a median 57.5% of the main stat (40–75%); median cost 55 mana at rank 1 (50–60) and 70 at rank 5 (60–80); median cooldown 14 s (12–15 s). Ultimates: median cost 80 mana (55–100) and cooldown 90 s (90–100 s). 272 counter lines were written by hand and 140 come from the stop-rule script.

### Cited Findings

**The wiki pages reflect the two newest patches.** Each balance change below, from the OB47 and OB48 notes, was looked up on the matching god page and the page shows the new value:
- OB48 (16–17 Nov 2026) ([smite2.live, OB48 notes](https://smite2.live/news/open-beta-48-notes)): Da Ji's One Thousand Cuts at 30% Strength and 15–35% mitigation; Baron Samedi's Vivid Gaze debuff at 4–6 s with 6% protections scaling; Hou Yi's Ricochet at 70% Intelligence; Ix Chel's Threads of Light cost 50–70 and Lunar Invocation cost 65–85; Nox's Siphon Darkness at 100% Intelligence ([S2 wiki, Da Ji](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Da_Ji); [Baron Samedi](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Baron_Samedi); [Hou Yi](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Hou_Yi); [Ix Chel](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Ix_Chel); [Nox](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Nox)).
- OB47 (2–3 Nov 2026) ([PatchBot, OB47 "The Night Update"](https://patchbot.io/games/smite-2/articles/2052-ob47-the-night-update)): Hou Yi's Ricochet at 85–265 damage, 60% Strength, flat 10 s; Hel's Decay at 60% Intelligence; Ne Zha's Wind Fire Wheels combo hits at 25–105 with 55% Strength; Nike's Rend at 50–150; Nut's Warp at 14–12 s; Ah Puch's Empty the Crypts at 20% Intelligence per tick; Hachiman's Eagle Eye at 115% basic attack damage ([S2 wiki, Hel](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Hel); [Ne Zha](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Ne_Zha); [Nike](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Nike); [Nut](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Nut); [Ah Puch](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Ah_Puch); [Hachiman](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Hachiman)).
- Other OB48 changes, also found on the pages: Izanami's aspect Dark Portal at 170–450 with 30% Intelligence and its Sickle Storm inner (root) radius 1.4 m; Cupid's aspect Lovestruck at 4% heal per stack; Nut's aspect Warp with 1 s immunity; Cerberus' aspect Ghastly Breath displacement immune while channelling ([smite2.live, OB48 notes](https://smite2.live/news/open-beta-48-notes); [S2 wiki, Izanami](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Izanami); [Cupid](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Cupid); [Nut](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Nut); [Cerberus](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Cerberus)). Nox's Siphon Darkness delay (0.7 → 0.4 s in OB48) is not listed as a number on the Nox page, so the 0.4 s comes from the notes only. Sol's Aspect of Conflagration rework was not checked against her page.
- The OB48 notes list no change to Purification Beads ([smite2.live, OB48 notes](https://smite2.live/news/open-beta-48-notes)).

**Per-god data** (numbers from the god page linked on each entry; counter lines are inference as explained at the top).

#### Smite 2 roster, A to Z

##### Achilles
Source: [S2 wiki, Achilles](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Achilles) (revision of 2026-10-11; raw text via [wiki API](https://wiki.smite2.com/api.php?action=query&prop=revisions&rvprop=content&rvslots=main&format=json&titles=Achilles)).
- **Basic attack:** Damage Scaling: 100% STR + 20% INT + 100% Attack Damage; Range: 2.56 m; Cone Angle: 120 deg.
- **P Gift of the Gods** *(passive)*. Health: 25 + 10 Per Level; Protections: 5 + 2 Per Level; STR: 3 + 1.5 Per Level; Movement Speed: 1% + 0.25% Per Level. *Counter:* passive; nothing to dodge, play around what triggers it.
- **1 Shield of Achilles** *(cone 80 deg)*. Damage: 100/155/210/265/320; Damage Scaling: 80% STR; Stun Duration: 0.8 s; Bonus Stun Duration: 0.2 s; Bonus Damage Scaling: 15% STR; Cone Angle: 80 deg; Small Radius: 3.2 m; Large Radius: 8 m; Cooldown: 14 s; Cost: 60/65/70/75/80 mana. Mechanics: enemy CC: stun. *Counter:* Short-range frontal stun: stay out of the close cone, and treat the weaker outer shock as chip damage; Beads cleanse the stun.
- **2 Radiant Glory** *(self buff / empowered attack)*. STR: 10%; Protections: 20%; Crowd Control Reduction: 20/22.5/25/27.5/30%; Buff Duration: 6.1 s; Heal: 16/18/20/22/24; Physical Shield Health: 75 + 20 Per Level; Protections Reduced: 1 Per Level; Debuff Duration: 3 s; Cooldown: 9 s; Cost: 40/45/50/55/60 mana; no damage values listed. *Counter (rule-based):* Self buff: back off and wait out its 6.1 s window, then trade.
- **3 Combat Dodge (Spear Strike)** *(dash)*. Damage: 65/105/145/185/225; Damage Scaling: 45% STR; Slow: 7.5% Per Stack; Slow Duration: 3 s; Dodge Range: 3.2 m; Spear Strike Range: 5.6 m; Spear Strike Radius: 0.96 m; Cooldown: 14/13.5/13/12.5/12 s; Cost: 22/24/26/28/30 mana. Mechanics: reactivation/refire; enemy CC: slow. *Counter:* Two parts: the dodge is free movement, then a narrow spear line; sidestep the thrust after the dodge. Cripple stops the dodge.
- **U Fatal Strike** *(projectile, dash)*. Damage: 180/270/360/450/540; Damage Scaling: 100% STR; Execute Health %: 35%; Increased Damage Taken: 5%; Debuff Duration: 10 s; Dash Range: 5.6 m; Dash Radius: 0.96 m; Cooldown: 90 s; Cost: 60/65/70/75/80 mana. Mechanics: passes minions, stops on the first god, walls block it; caster CC immune; reactivation/refire. *Counter:* The brief wind-up is the tell: stay above the execute threshold and out of the dash line; a wall or a god in front stops the dash. He is CC immune during it.
- *Aspect version (numbers as listed under the page's God Aspect section):* 2 Radiant Glory: STR: 10%; Buff Duration: 6.1 s; Heal: 16/18/20/22/24; Physical Shield Health: 75 + 20 Per Level; Protections Reduced: 1 Per Level; Debuff Duration: 3 s; Cooldown: 9 s; Cost: 40/45/50/55/60 mana | 3 Combat Dodge (Spear Strike): Damage: 65/105/145/185/225; Damage Scaling: 45% STR; Slow: 7.5% Per Stack; Slow Duration: 3 s; Bonus Damage Scaling: 15% STR; Dodge Range: 3.2 m; Spear Strike Range: 5.6 m; Spear Strike Radius: 0.96 m; Cooldown: 14/13.5/13/12.5/12 s; Cost: 22/24/26/28/30 mana.

##### Agni
Source: [S2 wiki, Agni](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Agni) (revision of 2026-11-16; raw text via [wiki API](https://wiki.smite2.com/api.php?action=query&prop=revisions&rvprop=content&rvslots=main&format=json&titles=Agni)).
- **Basic attack:** Damage Scaling: 100% STR + 20% INT + 100% Attack Damage; Range: 8.8 m; Radius: 0.48 m.
- **P Combustion** *(passive)*. Damage: 5; Damage Scaling: 6% INT; Debuff Duration: 3 s; Hot Streak Duration: 10 s. *Counter:* passive; nothing to dodge, play around what triggers it.
- **1 Noxious Fumes** *(placed object/summon)*. Damage: 10/20/30/40/50; Damage Scaling: 5% INT; Explode Damage: 20/40/60/80/100; Explode Damage Scaling: 20% INT; Stun Duration: 0.8 s; Healing Reduction: -40%; Healing Reduction Duration: 5 s; Range: 8.8 m; Radius: 3.2 m; Cooldown: 13 s; Cost: 60/65/70/75/80 mana. Mechanics: enemy CC: stun. *Counter:* Walk out of the cloud before he touches it with another ability (that detonation stuns); Beads cleanse the stun.
- **2 Flame Wave** *(piercing line)*. Damage: 100/155/210/265/320; Damage Scaling: 65% INT; Range: 8 m; Radius: 2.59 m; Cooldown: 15/14.5/14/13.5/13 s; Cost: 60/65/70/75/80 mana. Mechanics: passes through all enemies and walls (cannot be blocked). *Counter (rule-based):* Unblockable: strafe out of its width (2.59 m) or stay beyond 8 m.
- **3 Path of Flames** *(dash)*. Damage 20/30/40/50/60; Damage Scaling: 15% INT; Range: 9.6 m; Radius: 0.8 m; Cooldown: 16 s; Cost: 70/75/80/85/90 mana. Mechanics: caster displacement immune. *Counter (rule-based):* Cripple prevents it.
- **U Rain Fire** *(placed object/summon)*. Damage: 115/160/205/250/295; Damage Scaling: 60% INT; Self Slow: 50%; Range: 10.4 m; Radius: 3.2 m; Cooldown: 20 s; Cost: 10 mana. *Counter:* Meteors land on small circles at the cursor: keep moving sideways, never stand still between charges.
- *Aspect version (numbers as listed under the page's God Aspect section):* P Combustion: Ignite Damage: 3; Ignite Scaling: 1.5% STR; Consumed Ignite Damage: 5; Consumed Ignite Scaling: 70% STR | U Rain Fire: Damage: 120/150/180/210/240; Damage Scaling: 20% INT; Self Slow: 50%; Range: 10.4 m; Radius: 3.2 m; Cooldown: 20 s; Cost: 10 mana.

##### Ah Puch
Source: [S2 wiki, Ah Puch](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Ah_Puch) (revision of 2026-11-03; raw text via [wiki API](https://wiki.smite2.com/api.php?action=query&prop=revisions&rvprop=content&rvslots=main&format=json&titles=Ah_Puch)).
- **Basic attack:** Damage Scaling: 100% STR + 20% INT + 100% Attack Damage; Range: 8.8 m; Radius: 0.48 m.
- **P Hollow Ground** *(passive)*. Cooldown Reduction: 1 s; Max Health Restore: 3%; Max Mana Restore: 3%. *Counter:* passive; nothing to dodge, play around what triggers it.
- **1 Undead Surge** *(projectile)*. Damage: 90/110/130/150/170; Damage Scaling: 35% INT; Slow: 20/25/30/35/40%; Slow Duration: 2 s; Healing Reduction: -25%; Healing Reduction Duration: 5 s; Range: 9.6 m; Radius: 3.2 m; Cooldown: 10 s; Cost: 60/65/70/75/80 mana. Mechanics: projectile, stops on the first unit (minion or god), walls block it; reactivation/refire; enemy CC: slow. *Counter:* Sidestep the wave, or keep a minion in front: it stops on the first unit. He can pop it early, so do not just outrun it.
- **2 Corpse Explosion** *(ground circle)*. Damage: 60/70/80/90/100; Damage Scaling: 35% INT; Corpse Damage: 55/80/105/130/155; Corpse Damage Scaling: 30% INT; Range: 8.8 m; Radius: 3.2 m; Cooldown: 10 s; Cost: 60 mana. *Counter (rule-based):* Placed at up to 8.8 m with no listed delay: keep juking so it lands where you were, and walk out of the 3.2 m area.
- **3 Fleeting Breath** *(ground circle)*. Tick Damage: 24/32/40/48/56; Tick Damage Scaling: 16% INT; Bonus Damage: 65/120/175/230/285; Bonus Damage Scaling: 50% INT; Debuff Duration: 4 s; Stun Duration: 1.5 s; Range: 8.8 m; Radius: 2.08 m; Cooldown: 10 s; Cost: 60/65/70/75/80 mana. Mechanics: enemy CC: stun, charm. *Counter (rule-based):* Placed at up to 8.8 m with no listed delay: keep juking so it lands where you were, and walk out of the 2.08 m area. Beads cleanse the stun/charm.
- **U Empty the Crypts** *(circle around the god)*. Damage Per Tick: 50/55/60/65/70; Damage Scaling Per Tick: 20% INT; Healing Reduction: 25% per stack; Damage Dealt Reduction: 3% per stack; Debuff Duration: 5 s; Max Stacks: 3; Radius: 16 m; Cooldown: 110 s; Cost: 60/65/70/75/80 mana. *Counter:* A large field at a location for 6 s: leave it at once; stacks build every tick you stay.

Damage: 150/250/350/450/550; Damage Scaling: 70% INT + 55% STR; Max Health Shield: 35%; Self CDR: 50/55/60/65/70; Enemy CDR: 50; Protections: 10/15/20/25/30; Lamp Interact Size: 4.5 m; Range: 13.6 m; Cooldown: 90 s; Cost: 70/80/90/00/110 mana. Mechanics: passes minions, stops on the first god, walls block it; enemy CC: pull. **Data flag:** Cost is not monotonic on the wiki (70/80/90/00/110 mana): probably a typo. *Counter:* Dodge the lamp throw or have another god body-block it; inside, survive until the exit opens.
- *Aspect:* none listed on the page.

##### Athena
Source: [S2 wiki, Athena](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Athena) (revision of 2026-10-11; raw text via [wiki API](https://wiki.smite2.com/api.php?action=query&prop=revisions&rvprop=content&rvslots=main&format=json&titles=Athena)).
- **Basic attack:** Damage Scaling: 100% STR + 20% INT + 100% Attack Damage; Range: 1.92 m; Cone Angle: 120 deg.
- **P Reach** *(passive, piercing line)*. Damage Scaling: 70% INT + 125% STR; Range: 8.8 m; Radius: 0.48 m. Mechanics: passes through all enemies and walls (cannot be blocked). *Counter:* passive; nothing to dodge, play around what triggers it.
- **1 Preemptive Strike** *(projectile, dash)*. Damage: 80/130/180/230/280; Damage Scaling: 55% STR; Slow: 20/22.5/25/27.5/30%; Slow Duration: 2 s; Protections: 15/20/25/30/35 + 10%; Buff Duration: 4 s; Range: 8.8 m; Radius: 0.96 m; Cooldown: 14 s; Cost: 40/50/60/70/80 mana. Mechanics: passes minions, stops on the first god, walls block it; enemy CC: slow. *Counter (rule-based):* Sidestep it; minions do not shield you, but a wall or another god does. Cripple prevents it; it stops on the first god, so a teammate in front absorbs it.
- **2 Confound** *(cone 90 deg)*. Damage: 40/65/90/115/140; Damage Scaling: 20% INT; Taunt Duration: 0.75/0.9/1.05/1.2/1.35 s; Taunt Duration Scaling: 0.2 s per 115 Item Protections, stacking up to 2 times; Range: 5.6 m; Cone Angle: 90 deg; Cooldown: 16/15.5/15/14.5/14 s; Cost: 60/65/79/75/80 mana. Mechanics: enemy CC: taunt. **Data flag:** Cost is not monotonic on the wiki (60/65/79/75/80 mana): probably a typo. *Counter:* Frontal taunt cone: stay beside or behind her; Beads cleanse the taunt.
- **3 Shield Wall** *(circle around the god, delay (length not listed))*. Initial Damage: 60/100/140/180/220; Initial Damage Scaling: 45% INT; Final Damage: 80/140/200/260/320; Final Damage Scaling: 55% INT; Slow Duration: 2 s; Radius: 3.2 m; Cooldown: 12 s; Cost: 50/60/70/80/90 mana. Mechanics: enemy CC: slow. *Counter (rule-based):* Keep more than 3.2 m from the god while it is active.
- **U Defender of Olympus** *(teleport, channel)*. Damage: 340/415/490/565/640; Damage Scaling: 90% INT; Shield Health: 200/225/250/275/300; Shield Health Scaling: 15% Max Health; Movement Speed: 10/12.5/15/17.5/20%; Cooldown: 120/115/110/105/100 s; Cost: 80/90/100/110/120 mana. Mechanics: caster CC immune. *Counter:* Watch for her ally's low health: the teleport has a cast time, so move away from the landing ally or punish her arrival.
- *Aspect version (numbers as listed under the page's God Aspect section):* 3 Shield Wall: Initial Damage: 60/100/140/180/220; Initial Damage Scaling: 45% INT; Final Damage: 80/140/200/260/320; Final Damage Scaling: 55% INT; Radius: 3.2 m; Cooldown: 12 s; Cost: 50/60/70/80/90 mana | U Defender of Olympus: Shield Health: 200/225/250/275/300; Shield Health Scaling: 15% Max Health; Increased Damage Taken: 10/12.5/15/17.5/20%; Cooldown: 120/115/110/105/100 s; Cost: 80/90/100/110/120 mana.

##### Bellona

- **Basic attack:** Damage Scaling: 100% STR + 20% INT + 100% Attack Damage; Scourge Heal Per Hit: 5/6/7/8/9; Scourge Heal Scaling Per Hit: 7% Physical Protection; Range: 1.92 m; Scourge Range: 2.56 m; Cone Angle: 120 deg.
- **P Master Of War** *(passive)*. Movement Speed: 1.5 Per Stack; Physical Protection: 4 Per Stack; Magical Protection: 4 Per Stack; STR: 3% Per Stack; Attack Speed: 5% Per Stack; Buff Duration: 7 s; Max Buff Stacks 5. *Counter:* passive; nothing to dodge, play around what triggers it.
- **1 Shield Bash** *(dash)*. Damage: 75/115/155/195/235; Damage Scaling: 50% STR + 30% Physical and Magical Protection; Reflect Damage: 25% of the damage blocked; Reflect Damage Scaling: 7% Physical and Magical Protection; Slow: 20%; Slow Duration: 1.5 s; Dash Range: 2.8 m; Bash Range: 4 m; Bash Cone Angle: 120 deg; Cooldown: 14 s; Cost: 50/55/60/65/70 mana. Mechanics: enemy CC: slow. *Counter (rule-based):* Cripple prevents it.
- **2 Bludgeon** *(self buff / empowered attack)*. Spin Damage: 55/90/125/165/200; Spin Damage Scaling: 25% STR; Slam Damage: 70/130/190/250/310; Slam Damage Scaling: 70% STR; Spin Radius: 4 m; Slam Range: 4.8 m; Slam Radius: 1.92 m; Cooldown: 14/13/12/11/10 s; Cost: 40/50/60/70/80 mana. *Counter (rule-based):* Self buff: back off and wait out its window, then trade.
- **3 Scourge** *(ground circle)*. Damage: 80/120/160/200/240; Damage Scaling: 50% STR; Heal: 6/7/8/9/10; Heal Scaling: 6% Physical Protection; Disarmed Duration: 1.25/1.4/1.55/1.7/1.85 s; Range: 8.8 m; Radius: 1.6 m; Cooldown: 16/15/14/13/12 s; Cost: 40/50/60/70/80 mana. Mechanics: enemy CC: disarm. *Counter:* Short frontal lash that disarms: stay out of melee range while it is ready.
- **U Eagle's Rally** *(leap)*. Damage: 300/370/440/510/580; Damage Scaling: 60% STR; Stun Duration: 0.75 s; STR: 8/16/24/32/40 + 7.5% Protections; INT: 22/30/38/46/54 + 7.5% Protections; Protection: 15/20/25/30/35 + 7.5% Physical and Magical Protections; Range: 8.8 m; Radius: 2.4 m; Cooldown: 90 s; Cost: 100 mana. Mechanics: caster CC immune; enemy CC: stun. *Counter (rule-based):* Cripple prevents it; it goes over units, so punish the landing spot. Beads cleanse the stun. The caster is CC immune during it: do not spend CC into it.
- *Aspect version (numbers as listed under the page's God Aspect section):* 1 Shield Bash: Damage: 75/115/155/195/235; Damage Scaling: 50% STR + 30% Physical and Magical Protection; Reflect Damage: 25% of the damage blocked; Reflect Damage Scaling: 7% Physical and Magical Protection; Slow: 20%; Slow Duration: 1.5 s; Dash Range: 2.8 m; Bash Range: 4 m; Bash Cone Angle: 120 deg; Ally Radius: 6 m; Cooldown: 14 s; Cost: 50/55/60/65/70 mana | 2 Bludgeon: Spin Damage: 55/90/125/165/200; Spin Damage Scaling: 25% STR; Slam Damage: 70/130/190/250/310; Slam Damage Scaling: 70% STR; Spin Radius: 4 m; Slam Range: 4.8 m; Slam Radius: 1.92 m; Cooldown: 14/13/12/11/10 s; Cost: 40/50/60/70/80 mana | 3 Scourge: Damage: 80/120/160/200/240; Damage Scaling: 50% STR; Heal: 6/7/8/9/10; Heal Scaling: 6% Physical Protection; Disarmed Duration: 1.25/1.4/1.55/1.7/1.85 s; Range: 8.8 m; Radius: 1.6 m; Ally Radius: 6 m; Cooldown: 16/15/14/13/12 s; Cost: 40/50/60/70/80 mana.

##### Cabrakan
Source: [S2 wiki, Cabrakan](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Cabrakan) (revision of 2026-11-16; raw text via [wiki API](https://wiki.smite2.com/api.php?action=query&prop=revisions&rvprop=content&rvslots=main&format=json&titles=Cabrakan)).
- **Basic attack:** Damage Scaling: 100% STR + 20% INT + 100% Attack Damage; Range: 1.92 m; Cone Angle: 120 deg.
- **P Shadow Zone** *(passive)*. Self Damage Mitigation: 4% + 1% per 100 Item Protections; Ally Damage Mitigation: 4%; Damage Increase: 3%; Debuff Duration: 4 s; Radius: 4.8 m. *Counter:* passive; nothing to dodge, play around what triggers it.
- **1 Seismic Crush** *(self buff / empowered attack)*. Damage: 90/140/190/240/290; Damage Scaling: 45% INT + 75% STR; Stun Duration: 0.8 s; Attack Speed: 70%; Movement Speed: 15/20/25/30/35%; Buff Duration: 5 s; Cooldown: 14/13.5/13/12.5/12 s; Cost: 50/55/60/65/70 mana. Mechanics: enemy CC: stun. *Counter:* His next basic attack stuns: stay out of melee while it is active, or Beads the stun.
- **2 Refraction Shield** *(cone 120 deg)*. Damage: 50/95/140/185/230; Damage Scaling: 55% INT; Enhanced Damage Scaling: 75% STR; Stun Duration: 0.9/1/1.1/1.2/1.3 s; Heal: 10/13/16/19/22 Per Target; Heal Scaling: 0.5% Max Health; Mana Restore: 10/14/18/22/26 Per Target; Protections: 5/6/7/8/9; Buff Duration: 8 s; Max Stacks: 5; Range: 3.2 m; Cone Angle: 120 deg; Cooldown: 12 s; Enhanced Cooldown: 12 s; Cost: 10 mana. Mechanics: reactivation/refire; enemy CC: stun. *Counter (rule-based):* Stay outside the 120 deg cone (3.2 m), or get beside or behind the god. Beads cleanse the stun.
- **3 Tremors** *(channel, circle around the god)*. Damage: 18/28/38/48/58; Damage Scaling: 40% INT; Refire Damage: 50/90/130/170/210; Refire Damage Scaling: 55% STR; Tremble Duration: 0.55 s; Cripple Duration: 1 s; Radius: 4.8 m; Cooldown: 4 s; Refire Cooldown: 12 s; Cost: 20/30/40/50/60 mana. Mechanics: reactivation/refire; enemy CC: pull, tremble, cripple, vortex. *Counter (rule-based):* Keep more than 4.8 m from the god while it is active. Interrupt the channel with hard CC, or leave the area. Beads cleanse the pull.
- **U Tectonic Shift** *(placed object/summon)*. Damage: 150/250/350/450/550; Damage Scaling: 65% INT + 95% STR; Range: 5.6 m; Radius: 4 m; Cooldown: 90/85/80/75/70 s; Cost: 50/55/60/65/70 mana. Mechanics: reactivation/refire. *Counter:* Watch for the fissure line: walls rise from it, so do not stand where they can trap you against him.

##### Cerberus
Source: [S2 wiki, Cerberus](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Cerberus) (revision of 2026-11-17; raw text via [wiki API](https://wiki.smite2.com/api.php?action=query&prop=revisions&rvprop=content&rvslots=main&format=json&titles=Cerberus)).
- **Basic attack:** Damage Scaling: 100% STR + 20% INT + 100% Attack Damage; Range: 1.92 m; Cone Angle: 120 deg.
- **P Spirit of Death** *(passive)*. no numbers listed on the wiki. *Counter:* passive; nothing to dodge, play around what triggers it.
- **1 Paralyzing Spit** *(piercing line)*. Damage: 55/80/105/130/155; Damage Scaling: 20% INT; Stun: 1.1/1.2/1.3/1.4/1.5 s; Range: 8 m; Radius: 0.56 m; Cooldown: 13/12.5/12/11.5/11 s; Cost: 60/65/70/75/80 mana. Mechanics: passes through all enemies and walls (cannot be blocked); enemy CC: stun. *Counter (rule-based):* Unblockable: strafe out of its width (0.56 m) or stay beyond 8 m. Beads cleanse the stun.
- **2 Ghastly Breath** *(cone 90 deg, channel)*. Damage Per Tick: 20/30/40/50/60; Damage Scaling Per Tick: 12.5% INT; Slow: 7/7.5/8/8.5/9%; Magical Protections Reduced: 3/4.5/6/7.5/9; Debuff Duration: 2 s; Range: 5.6 m; Cone Angle: 90 deg; Slow Cone Angle: 30 deg; Cooldown: 14 s; Cost: 60/65/70/75/80 mana. Mechanics: enemy CC: slow. *Counter (rule-based):* Stay outside the 90 deg cone (5.6 m), or get beside or behind the god. Interrupt the channel with hard CC, or leave the area.
- **3 Soul Expulsion** *(leap)*. Damage: 80/120/160/200/240; Damage Scaling: 60% INT; Heal From Gods: 15/30/45/60/75; Heal From Minions: 10/15/20/25/30; Soul Health: 50/60/70/80/90; Range: 8.8 m; Radius: 2.4 m; Cooldown: 15 s; Cost: 55 mana. *Counter (rule-based):* Cripple prevents it; it goes over units, so punish the landing spot.
- **U Stygian Torment** *(circle around the god)*. Damage: 180/255/330/405/480; Damage Scaling: 55% INT; Radius: 4.8 m; Displacement Distance: 3.6 m; Cooldown: 105 s; Cost: 60/65/70/75/80 mana. Mechanics: enemy CC: pull, displacement. *Counter (rule-based):* Keep more than 4.8 m from the god while it is active. Beads cleanse the pull/displacement.
- *Aspect version (numbers as listed under the page's God Aspect section):* 2 Ghastly Breath: Damage Per Tick: 20/30/40/50/60; Damage Scaling Per Tick: 12.5% INT; Slow: 7/7.5/8/8.5/9%; Magical Protections Reduced: 3/4.5/6/7.5/9; Debuff Duration: 2 s; Range: 5.6 m; Cone Angle: 90 deg; Slow Cone Angle: 30 deg; Cooldown: 14 s; Cost: 60/65/70/75/80 mana | 3 Soul Expulsion: Damage: 80/120/160/200/240; Damage Scaling: 60% INT; Soul Health: 50/60/70/80/90; Range: 8.8 m; Radius: 2.4 m; Cooldown: 15 s; Cost: 55 mana | U Stygian Torment: Damage: 180/255/330/405/480; Damage Scaling: 55% INT; Radius: 4.8 m; Cooldown: 105 s; Cost: 60/65/70/75/80 mana.

- **Basic attack:** Damage Scaling: 100% STR + 20% INT + 100% Attack Damage; Range: 8.8 m; Radius: 0.48 m.
- **P Heavy Glaive** *(passive, cone 120 deg)*. Damage Scaling: 30% STR + 5% INT + 30% Attack Damage; Range: 1.92 m; Cone Angle: 120 deg. *Counter:* passive; nothing to dodge, play around what triggers it.
- **1 Shifter Of Seasons** *(self buff / empowered attack)*. Spring Lifesteal: 3/3.5/4/4.5/5%; Spring Heal Scaling: 10% INT; Summer Damage: 5/10/15/20/25; Summer Damage Scaling: 12/15/18/21/24% STR or INT (whichever is higher); Fall Physical Protection Reduced: 5/10/15/20/25; Fall Magical Protection Reduced: 5/10/15/20/25; Debuff Scaling: 5% INT; Fall Debuff Duration: 10 s; Winter Slow: 10/11/12/13/14%; Winter Slow Scaling: 2.5% INT; Winter Slow Duration: 2 s; no mana cost listed (uses another resource or none). Mechanics: enemy CC: slow. *Counter (rule-based):* Self buff: back off and wait out its window, then trade.
- **2 Bramble Blast** *(ground circle)*. Initial Damage: 65/110/155/200/245; Initial Damage Scaling: 75% STR + 50% INT; Damage Per Tick: 8/12/16/20/24; Damage Scaling Per Tick: 6% STR + 12% INT; Root Duration: 1 s; Range: 8.8 m; Radius: 3.2 m; Cooldown: 15 s; Cost: 50/60/70/80/90 mana. Mechanics: 10 hits over 5 s; enemy CC: root. *Counter:* Explodes on the first hit or at max range, then roots: sidestep, and step out of the bramble area.
- **3 Horn Charge** *(dash)*. Damage: 90/140/190/240/290; Damage Scaling: 85% STR; Range: 8.8 m; Radius: 1.6 m; Cooldown: 15/14.5/14/13.5/13 s; Cost: 70/75/80/85/90 mana. *Counter (rule-based):* Cripple prevents it; it passes through enemies, so body-blocking does not work.
- **U The Wild Hunt** *(ground circle)*. Damage: 240/320/400/480/540; Damage Scaling: 80% STR + 100% INT; Polymorph Duration: 1.5 s; Slow: 20%; Slow Duration: 1.5 s; Range: 8.8 m; Radius: 4 m; Cooldown: 90 s; Cost: 100 mana. Mechanics: enemy CC: polymorph, slow. *Counter (rule-based):* Placed at up to 8.8 m with no listed delay: keep juking so it lands where you were, and walk out of the 4 m area. Beads cleanse the polymorph.

- **1 Thunder Strike** *(ground circle)*. Damage: 100/160/220/280/340; Damage Scaling: 75% STR + 60% INT; Axe Duration: 5 s; Range: 9.6 m; Radius: 3.2 m; Cooldown: 10 s; Cost: 45/50/55/60/65 mana. - **2 Torrent** *(dash, teleport)*. Damage: 100/160/220/280/340; Damage Scaling: 70% STR; Dash Damage: 35/57.5/80/102.5/125; Dash Damage Scaling: 30% STR; Protections: 5/7/9/11/13 Per Stack; Protection Buff Scaling: 6% INT; Buff Duration: 6 s; Max Buff Stacks: 4; Attack Radius: 3.2 m; Dash Radius: 1.28 m; Cooldown: 10/9.5/9/8.5/8 s; Cost: 50/60/70/80/90 mana. *Counter:* He can teleport to his thrown axe and spin: stay away from where the axe landed.

Mechanics: caster CC immune; enemy CC: knockup, silence. *Counter (rule-based):* Keep more than 5.6 m from the god while it is active. ##### Chronos
Source: [S2 wiki, Chronos](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Chronos) (revision of 2026-10-06; raw text via [wiki API](https://wiki.smite2.com/api.php?action=query&prop=revisions&rvprop=content&rvslots=main&format=json&titles=Chronos)).
- **Basic attack:** Damage Scaling: 100% STR + 20% INT + 100% Attack Damage; Range: 8.8 m; Radius: 0.48 m.
- **P Time Lord** *(passive)*. INT: 5 Per Stack; Max Stacks: 25. *Counter:* passive; nothing to dodge, play around what triggers it.
- **1 Time Rift** *(ground circle)*. Damage: 80/125/170/215/260; Damage Scaling: 80% INT; Range: 8.8 m; Radius: 1.6 m; Cooldown: 5 s; Cost: 50/55/60/65/70 mana. *Counter (rule-based):* Placed at up to 8.8 m with no listed delay: keep juking so it lands where you were, and walk out of the 1.6 m area.
- **2 Accelerate** *(self buff / empowered attack)*. Initial Movement Speed: 10%; Additional Movement Speed Over Time: 12/14/16/18/20%; Attack Speed: 25/27.5/30/32.5/35%; Heal: 1% Max Health; INT: 30%; INT Attack Contribution: +35%; Buff Duration: 7 s; Permanent INT Per Stack: 2; Permanent Attack Speed Per Stack: 0.5%; Cooldown: 12 s; Cost: 60 mana; no damage values listed. *Counter (rule-based):* Self buff: back off and wait out its 7 s window, then trade.
- **3 Stop Time** *(piercing line, delay 1 s)*. Damage: 50/75/100/125/150; Damage Scaling: 45% INT; Attack Speed Slow: 20%; Stun Duration: 1 s; Range: 11.2 m; Radius: 1.2 m; Cooldown: 16/15.5/15/14.5/14 s; Cost: 65/70/75/80/85 mana. Mechanics: passes through all enemies and walls (cannot be blocked); enemy CC: stun, slow. *Counter:* Gears pass through units: strafe out, or cleanse before the slow ramps into a stun.
- **U Rewind** *(teleport)*. Time Rewound: 8 s; Cooldown: 100/95/90/85/80 s; Cost: 50/55/60/65/70 mana; no damage values listed. Mechanics: caster CC immune. *Counter:* He returns to a recent spot with old health: push him hard just after he uses it, when it is on cooldown.
- *Aspect version (numbers as listed under the page's God Aspect section):* P Time Lord: no numbers | 1 Time Rift: Damage: 70/115/160/205/250; Damage Scaling: 60% INT; Range: 8.8 m; Radius: 1.6 m; Cooldown: 5 s; Cost: 50/55/60/65/70 mana | 2 Accelerate: Initial Movement Speed: 10%; Additional Movement Speed Over Time: 12/14/16/18/20%; Protections: 10/15/20/25/30%; Heal: 1% Max Health; Mana Regen: 3/4/5/6/7; Bonus INT: 15/25/35/45/55; Bonus STR: 10/15/20/25/30; Buff Duration: 7 s; Cooldown: 12 s; Cost: 60 mana | 3 Stop Time: Damage: 50/75/100/125/150; Damage Scaling: 45% INT; Attack Speed Slow: 20%; Stun Duration: 1 s; Attack Speed: 20%; Buff Duration: 3 s; Range: 11.2 m; Radius: 1.2 m; Cooldown: 16/15.5/15/14.5/14 s; Cost: 65/70/75/80/85 mana | U Rewind: Time Rewound: 8 s; Cooldown: 100/95/90/85/80 s; Cost: 50/55/60/65/70 mana.

##### Cu Chulainn

- **1 Barbed Spear** (Human form) *(piercing line)*. Damage: 90/150/210/270/330; Damage Scaling: 65% STR; Stun Duration: 2 s; Healing Reduction: -40%; Healing Reduction Duration: 5 s; Minion Rage Gain: 7; God Rage Gain: 10; Max Rage Gain: 24; Range: 7.2 m; Cooldown: 16/15/14/13/12 s; Cost: 9 Rage. Mechanics: passes through all enemies, stopped by walls; enemy CC: stun. *Counter (rule-based):* Passes through units: strafe out of the line or put a wall between you. Beads cleanse the stun.
- **2 Vent Anger** (Human form) *(area, walls stop it)*. Damage: 7/10/17/24/31/38; Damage Scaling: 5% STR; Movement Speed: 5/5/6/7/8/9%; Radius: 3.6 m; Cooldown: 6 s; Cost: 16 Rage. Mechanics: hits everything in its area, walls stop it. *Counter (rule-based):* Walls cut it off: stand behind terrain or leave the area.
- **3 Salmon's Leap** (Human form) *(piercing line, leap)*. Damage: 80/135/190/245/300; Damage Scaling: 60% STR; Minion Rage Gain: 5; God Rage Gain: 8; Max Rage Gain: 18; Leap Range: 5.6 m; Slam Range: 3.2 m; Radius: 2.4 m; Cooldown: 15 s; Cost: 9 Rage. Mechanics: passes through all enemies and walls (cannot be blocked). *Counter:* Leap and slam: cripple denies it; move off the landing spot and out of the slam in front of him.

Mechanics: hits everything in its area, walls stop it; enemy CC: knockup. *Counter (rule-based):* Walls cut it off: stand behind terrain or leave the area. Interrupt the channel with hard CC, or leave the area. Beads cleanse the knockup.
- **1 Ground Slam** (Berserk form) *(piercing line)*. Damage: 90/150/210/270/330; Damage Scaling: 65% STR; Root Duration: 1/1.1/1.2/1.3/1.4 s; Range: 7.2 m; Cooldown: 16/15/14/13/12 s; no mana cost listed (uses another resource or none). - **2 Vent Anger** (Berserk form) *(area, walls stop it)*. Mechanics: hits everything in its area, walls stop it. *Counter (rule-based):* Walls cut it off: stand behind terrain or leave the area.

Mechanics: enemy CC: slow. *Counter (rule-based):* Self buff: back off and wait out its window, then trade.
- **2 One Thousand Cuts** *(cone 180 deg, channel)*. Damage: 35/50/65/80/95; Damage Scaling: 30% STR; Movement Speed: 30%; Damage Mitigation: 15/20/25/30/35%; Range: 3.2 m; Cone Angle: 180 deg; Cooldown: 11 s; Cost: 60/65/70/75/80 mana. *Counter (rule-based):* Stay outside the 180 deg cone (3.2 m), or get beside or behind the god. - **3 Trickster Spirit** *(teleport, delay (length not listed))*. Damage: 60/95/130/165/200; Damage Scaling: 70% STR; Range: 8.8 m; Radius: 1.6 m; Cooldown: 15 s; Cost: 60/65/70/75/80 mana. *Counter:* She teleports to a target after a short delay: move off the targeted spot or ward before she arrives.
- **U Paolao** *(ground circle)*. Damage: 100/125/150/175/200; Damage Scaling: 60% STR; Damage Per Tick: 10/15/20/25/30; Damage Scaling Per Tick: 5% STR; Stun Duration: 0.5 s; Range: 12.8 m; Radius: 1.6 m; Pulse Radius: 3.2 m; Cooldown: 120 s; Cost: 50 mana. Mechanics: caster CC immune; enemy CC: stun, cripple, vortex. *Counter:* Chains from above while she is CC immune: keep cover between you, Beads before the stun lands.

- **P Mark of Vengeance** *(passive)*. Attack Speed: 5% per stack; Movement Speed: 20%; STR: 5 per stack; Attack Speed Duration: 4 s; Movement Speed Duration: 5 s; Max Attack Speed Stacks: 6; Max STR Stacks: 5; Cooldown: 20 s. *Counter:* passive; nothing to dodge, play around what triggers it.
- **1 Imbue Arrows** *(self buff / empowered attack)*. Strike Shot Bonus Damage: 7/14/21/28/35; Spread Shot Side Arrow Damage: 9%; Storm Shot Attack Speed Bonus: 20/25/30/35/40%; Storm Shot Projectile Speed: 120%; Buff Duration: 6 s; Storm Shot Attack Damage: -10%; Strike Shot Range: 7.52 m; Strike Shot Radius: 1.6 m; Storm Shot Range: 12.8 m; Cooldown: 12 s; Cost: 60/65/70/75/80 mana. *Counter (rule-based):* Self buff: back off and wait out its 6 s window, then trade.
- **2 Rolling Thunder** *(aimed line of strikes (wiki lists Radius > Range))*. Damage: 60/85/110/135/160; Damage Scaling: 40% STR; Mesmerize Duration: 0.75 s; Range: 2.4 m; Radius: 8.8 m; Cooldown: 13.5 s; Cost: 55/60/65/70/75 mana. Mechanics: enemy CC: mesmerize. **Data flag:** the wiki lists Range 2.4 m and Radius 8.8 m; the two labels look swapped (unconfirmed). *Counter:* Four strikes along an aimed line; being hit by two or more mesmerizes: strafe sideways off the line.
- **3 Jolt** *(dash)*. Dash Damage: 80/120/160/200/240; Dash Damage Scaling: 60% STR; Arrow Damage: 20/30/40/50/60; Arrow Damage Scaling: 120% Basic Attack Damage; Range: 2.4 m; Arrow Radius: 1.6 m; Cooldown: 15/14.5/14/13.5/13 s; Cost: 60 mana. Mechanics: enemy CC: root. *Counter (rule-based):* Cripple prevents it. Beads cleanse the root.
- **U Blades of Retribution** *(placed object/summon)*. Damage: 90/115/140/165/190; Damage Scaling: 55% STR; Damage Reduction: -40% per sword, up to a max of -80%; Cripple Duration: 1.25 s; Stun Duration: 1 s; Range: 12.8 m; Cooldown: 90 s; Cost: 100 mana. Mechanics: reactivation/refire; enemy CC: stun, cripple. *Counter:* Swords fly out in a cone (cripple) and can be recalled within 3 s; being hit by both passes stuns: after the first pass, move out of the line back to her.

##### Ix Chel
Source: [S2 wiki, Ix Chel](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Ix_Chel) (revision of 2026-11-17; raw text via [wiki API](https://wiki.smite2.com/api.php?action=query&prop=revisions&rvprop=content&rvslots=main&format=json&titles=Ix_Chel)).
- **Basic attack:** Damage Scaling: 100% STR + 20% INT + 100% Attack Damage; Range: 8.8 m; Radius: 0.48 m.
- **P Rainbow Weaver** *(passive)*. Bonus Damage: 40% INT; Range: 11.2 m; Radius: 1.28 m. *Counter:* passive; nothing to dodge, play around what triggers it.
- **1 Gleaming Blast** *(projectile)*. Damage: 80/100/120/140/160; Damage Scaling: 60% INT; Range: 11.2 m; Cooldown: 6 s; Cost: 40/45/50/55/60 mana. Mechanics: passes minions and walls, stops on the first god; enemy CC: blind. *Counter (rule-based):* Sidestep it; it ignores minions and walls, so only another god can absorb it.
- **1-alt Threads of Light** *(cone 19.6 deg)*. Damage: 60/105/150/195/240; Damage Scaling: 80% INT; Tip Damage: +25%; Blind Duration: 1 s; Range: 10 m; Cone Angle: 19.6 deg; Cooldown: 6 s; Cost: 50/55/60/65/70 mana. Mechanics: enemy CC: blind. *Counter:* Blinds those facing the strands: turn away or step out of the narrow cone.
- **2 Lunar Invocation** *(ground circle, delay (length not listed))*. Damage: 80/145/195/245/295; Damage Scaling: 60% INT; Heal: 50/85/120/155/180; Heal Scaling: 10% INT; Range: 8.8 m; Radius: 3.2 m; Cooldown: 14 s; Cost: 65/70/75/80/85 mana. Mechanics: enemy CC: knockup. *Counter:* Explodes after a brief delay: step out of the circle as soon as it appears; Beads cleanse the knock-up.
- **3 Rainbow Crash** *(ground circle)*. Damage: 80/120/160/200/240; Damage Scaling: 85% INT; Movement Speed: 20%; Root Duration: 1.25 s; Range: 8.8 m; Radius: 2.4 m; Cooldown: 14 s; Cost: 50/55/60/65/70 mana. Mechanics: enemy CC: root. *Counter:* Arcing shot that roots where it lands: step away from the landing spot.
- **U Great End** *(channel, ground circle)*. Damage Per Tick: 23/35/47/59/71; Damage Scaling Per Tick: 8% INT; Heal Per Tick: 2% of Missing Health; Echo: 20; Range: 11.2 m; Radius: 0.4 m; Cooldown: 110 s; Cost: 90 mana. *Counter:* CC-immune beam: strafe out of the line.
- *Aspect version (numbers as listed under the page's God Aspect section):* 1 Gleaming Blast: Shield Health Scaling: 80% INT; Shield Health: 180/220/240/280/320; Shield Duration: 3 s; Slow: 30/37.5/45/52.5/60%; Slow Duration: 3 s; Range: 11.2 m; Cooldown: 8 s; Cost: 40/45/50/55/60 mana | 2 Lunar Invocation: Damage: 80/145/195/245/295; Damage Scaling: 60% INT; Heal: 40/70/100/130/160; Heal Scaling: 40% INT; Range: 8.8 m; Radius: 3.2 m; Cooldown: 14 s; Cost: 65/70/75/80/85 mana | U Great End: Heal Per Tick: 30/50/70/90/110; Heal Scaling: 20% INT; Range: 11.2 m; Radius: 0.4 m; Cooldown: 110 s; Cost: 90 mana.

- **P Death Draws Nigh** *(passive)*. Percent Penetration: 0.3% Per Stack; INT: 0.5 Per Stack; INT Per Level: 0.05 Per Stack; Max Stacks: 50. *Counter:* passive; nothing to dodge, play around what triggers it.
- **1 Sickle Storm** *(self buff / empowered attack)*. Attack Speed: 15/20/25/30/35%; Damage Scaling: 100% STR + 20% INT; Bonus Structure Damage: 5/10/15/20/25; Bleed Damage: 5/10/15/20/25 + 30% INT over 4 ticks; Max Debuff Stacks: 3; Debuff Duration: 2 s; Cooldown: 11 s; Cost: 60/65/70/75/80 mana. *Counter (rule-based):* Self buff: back off and wait out its window, then trade.
- **2 Spectral Projection** *(piercing line)*. Damage: 50/90/130/170/210; Damage Scaling: 80% STR + 75% INT; Slow: 20/22.5/25/27.5/30%; Slow Duration: 2 s; Healing Reduction: -40%; Healing Reduction Duration: 5 s; Range: 11.2 m; Radius: 0.8 m; Cooldown: 14/13.5/13/12.5/12 s; Cost: 50/55/60/65/70 mana. Mechanics: passes through all enemies and walls (cannot be blocked); enemy CC: slow. *Counter (rule-based):* Unblockable: strafe out of its width (0.8 m) or stay beyond 11.2 m.
- **3 Fade Away** *(leap, stealth)*. Movement Speed: 15/20/25/30/35%; Buff Duration: 3/3.5/4/4.5/5 s; Range: 8.8 m; Cooldown: 17/16.5/16/15.5/15 s; Cost: 50/55/60/65/70 mana; no damage values listed. *Counter (rule-based):* Cripple prevents it; it goes over units, so punish the landing spot. Damage partly reveals it and hard CC breaks it; sweep the area where it vanished.
- **U Dark Portal** *(placed object/summon)*. Damage: 180/260/340/420/500; Damage Scaling: 100% STR + 30% INT; Damage Over Time: 50/80/110/140/170 over 4 ticks; Damage Over Time Scaling: 70% INT over 4 ticks; Silence Duration: 1.75 s; Initial Slow Amount: 60%; Range: 9.6 m; Radius: 2.4 m; Cooldown: 90 s; Cost: 100 mana. Mechanics: enemy CC: silence, slow. *Counter:* Leave the circle; Beads cleanse the silence.

Source: [S2 wiki, Jormungandr](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Jormungandr) (revision of 2026-10-06; raw text via [wiki API](https://wiki.smite2.com/api.php?action=query&prop=revisions&rvprop=content&rvslots=main&format=json&titles=Jormungandr)).

- **P Immovable** *(passive)*. Damage Scaling: 6% Max Health or 115% STR (whichever is higher); Slow: 15%; Increased Damage Taken: 5%; Dazed Duration: 2 s; Debuff Max Stacks: 14; Debuff Duration: 6 s. *Counter:* passive; nothing to dodge, play around what triggers it.
- **1 Venomous Haze** *(ground circle)*. Damage: 80/120/160/200/240; Damage Scaling: 40% INT; Damage Per Tick: 10/20/30/40/50; Damage Scaling Per Tick: 5% INT; Slow: 20%; Slow Duration: 1.5 s; Healing Reduction: -40%; Healing Reduction Duration: 5 s; Extra Cooling: 25% Base Rate; Range: 12 m; Inner Radius: 1.6 m; Outer Radius: 2.4 m; Cooldown: 13/12.5/12/11.5/11 s; Cost: 50/55/60/65/70 mana. Mechanics: enemy CC: slow. *Counter:* Walk out of the clouds; they linger 20 s, so do not fight inside them.
- **2 Consuming Bellow** *(channel, circle around the god)*. Damage: 120/180/240/300/360; Damage Scaling: 45% INT + 30% STR; Bonus Damage: 20/25/30/35/40; Bonus Damage Scaling: 10% INT; Slow: 30%; Debuff Duration: 1.5 s; Inner Radius: 3.2 m; Outer Radius: 4.8 m; Cooldown: 12 s; Cost: 70 mana. Mechanics: enemy CC: tremble, slow. *Counter:* Short channel then a roar around him: back off during the channel or interrupt it with hard CC.
- **3 Submerge** *(stealth, circle around the god)*. Damage: 70/125/180/235/290; Damage Scaling: 50% INT + 40% STR; Movement Speed: 35%; Max Submerge Duration: 5 s; Radius: 3.2 m; Pulse Radius: 20 m; Cooldown: 14 s; Cost: 70/75/80/85/90 mana. Mechanics: reactivation/refire; enemy CC: knockup. *Counter:* He is stealthed and moving: watch the area near him and expect a knock-up where he surfaces.
- **U The World Serpent** *(leap)*. Damage: 150/200/250/300/350; Damage Scaling: 35% INT + 30% STR; Emerge/Crash Damage: 75/100/125/150/175; Emerge/Crash Damage Scaling: 20% INT + 15% STR; Protections: 10/12/14/16/18; Max Protections Stacks: 4; Buff Duration 6 s; Range: 16 m; Radius: 3.2 m; Cooldown: 90 s; Cost: 50/55/60/65/70 mana. Mechanics: caster CC immune; enemy CC: knockup. *Counter:* Up to 3 dives: each landing knocks up, so keep moving and spread out.

##### Kukulkan
Source: [S2 wiki, Kukulkan](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Kukulkan) (revision of 2026-11-16; raw text via [wiki API](https://wiki.smite2.com/api.php?action=query&prop=revisions&rvprop=content&rvslots=main&format=json&titles=Kukulkan)).
- **Basic attack:** Damage Scaling: 100% STR + 20% INT + 100% Attack Damage; Range: 8.8 m; Radius: 0.48 m.
- **P Power of the Wind Jewel** *(passive)*. INT From Item Mana: 7%. *Counter:* passive; nothing to dodge, play around what triggers it.
- **1 Zephyr** *(projectile)*. Damage: 85/135/185/235/285; Damage Scaling: 85% INT; Slow: 20/25/30/35/40%; Slow Duration: 3 s; Range: 11.2 m; Radius: 0.64 m; Explosion Radius: 2.25 m; Cooldown: 7 s; Cost: 50/55/60/65/70 mana. Mechanics: projectile, stops on the first unit (minion or god), walls block it; enemy CC: slow. *Counter:* Sidestep it; it explodes on the first unit, so do not stand next to minions.
- **2 Slipstream** *(piercing line, dash)*. Movement Speed: 7/9/11/13/15% Per Stack; Buff Duration: 4 s; Dash Damage: 95/145/195/245/295; Dash Damage Scaling: 90% INT; Dash Slow: 20/25/30/35/40%; Dash Slow Duration: 3 s; Dash Range: 8.8 m; Dash Radius: 2 m; Cooldown: 13/12.5/12/11.5/11 s; Cost: 40/45/50/55/60 mana. Mechanics: passes through all enemies, stopped by walls; enemy CC: slow. *Counter:* Speed buff, and a dash inside his whirlwind: cripple stops the dash.
- **3 Whirlwind** *(ground circle)*. Damage Per Tick: 12/19/26/33/40; Damage Scaling Per Tick: 20% INT; Range: 8.8 m; Radius: 3.2 m; Cooldown: 15/14/13/12/11 s; Cost: 50/60/70/80/90 mana. Mechanics: 6 hits over 2.5 s. *Counter:* Leave the whirlwind area; it spreads to enemies who enter.
- **U Spirit of the Nine Winds** *(piercing line)*. Damage: 400/500/600/700/800; Damage Scaling: 115% INT; Range: 20.8 m; Radius: 1.4 m; Cooldown: 90 s; Cost: 120 mana. Mechanics: passes through all enemies and walls (cannot be blocked); enemy CC: knockback. *Counter:* Long, unblockable tornado: strafe out of its path.
- *Aspect version (numbers as listed under the page's God Aspect section):* P Power of the Wind Jewel: INT From Item Mana: 7%; Bonus Max Mana: 5 from god kills and assists; Bonus Max Mana: 1 from jungle monster kills | 1 Zephyr: Damage: 85/140/195/250/305; Damage Scaling: 90% INT; Subsequent Damage: 40%; Slow: 10/15/20/25/30%; Slow Duration: 3 s; Range: 7 m; Radius: 0.64 m; Explosion Radius: 2.25 m; Cooldown: 7 s; Cost: 50/55/60/65/70 mana | 2 Slipstream: Dash Damage: 80/125/170/215/260; Dash Damage Scaling: 65% INT; Movement Speed: 7/9/11/13/15% Per Stack; Buff Duration: 4 s; Slow: 20/25/30/35/40%; Protections Reduced: : 5/10/15/20/25; Debuff Duration: 3 s; Dash Range: 8.8 m; Dash Radius: 2 m; Cooldown: 13/12.5/12/11.5/11 s; Cost: 40/45/50/55/60 mana | 3 Whirlwind: Damage: 50/75/100/125/150; Damage Scaling: 35% INT; Damage Per Tick: 10/15/20/25/30; Damage Scaling Per Tick: 10% INT; Radius: 3.2 m; Cooldown: 15/14/13/12/11 s; Cost: 50/60/70/80/90 mana.

##### Kumbhakarna
Source: [S2 wiki, Kumbhakarna](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Kumbhakarna) (revision of 2026-11-16; raw text via [wiki API](https://wiki.smite2.com/api.php?action=query&prop=revisions&rvprop=content&rvslots=main&format=json&titles=Kumbhakarna)).
- **Basic attack:** Damage Scaling: 100% STR + 20% INT + 100% Attack Damage; Range: 1.92 m; Cone Angle: 120 deg.
- **P Sleepy** *(passive)*. Health Regen: 4; Sleep Duration: 4 s; Attacks to Kill: 6 + 1 Per 5 Levels; Wake Health and Mana: 40%; Cooldown: 120 s. Mechanics: caster CC immune. *Counter:* When he falls asleep only god basic attacks hurt him: keep basic-attacking, or disengage.
- **1 Throw Back** *(dash)*. Dash Damage: 95/160/225/290/355; Dash Damage Scaling: 50% INT; Thrown Minion Damage: 95/165/235/305/375; Thrown Minion Damage Scaling: 60% INT; Dash Range: 7.2 m; Throw Range: 12 m; Cooldown: 10 s; Cost: 55/60/65/70/75 mana. *Counter:* Dash to the first enemy: keep minions between you; thrown minions are projectiles you can sidestep.
- **2 Groggy Strike** *(delay (length not listed))*. Damage: 100/155/210/265/320; Damage Scaling: 50% INT; Root Duration: 1.5 s; Range: 6.4 m; Cooldown: 10 s; Cost: 50/55/60/65/70 mana. Mechanics: caster displacement immune; enemy CC: root. *Counter:* Wind-up smash in front of him: step out of the line during the wind-up; Beads cleanse the root.
- **3 Mighty Yawn** *(circle around the god)*. Wake Damage: 40/75/110/145/180; Wake Damage Scaling: 25% INT; Mesmerize Duration: 1.5/1.75/2/2.25/2.5 s; Wake Slow: 20/22.5/25/27.5/30%; Wake Attack Speed Reduction: 20/22.5/25/27.5/30%; Debuff Duration: 2 s; Radius: 6.4 m; Cooldown: 15 s; Cost: 50 mana. Mechanics: enemy CC: mesmerize, slow. *Counter:* Mesmerize around him, even while asleep: keep your distance; damage wakes you early.
- **U Epic Uppercut** *(dash)*. Dash Damage: 75/100/125/150/175; Dash Damage Scaling: 15% INT; Landing Damage: 200/290/380/470/560; Landing Damage Scaling: 40% INT; Dash Range: 4.8 m; Landing Radius: 3.2 m; Cooldown: 90/85/80/75/70 s; Cost: 55/60/65/70/75 mana. Mechanics: caster CC immune; enemy CC: knockback. *Counter:* Dash that uppercuts the first god: sidestep or body-block it; he is CC immune during it.
- *Aspect:* none listed on the page.

##### Kuzenbo
Source: [S2 wiki, Kuzenbo](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Kuzenbo) (revision of 2026-11-17; raw text via [wiki API](https://wiki.smite2.com/api.php?action=query&prop=revisions&rvprop=content&rvslots=main&format=json&titles=Kuzenbo)).
- **Basic attack:** Damage Scaling: 100% STR + 20% INT + 100% Attack Damage; Range: 1.92 m; Cone Angle: 120 deg.
- **P Water Bowl** *(passive)*. Damage Mitigation: 1.5% per 2% of Max HP Taken; Max Stacks: 10; Buff Duration: 30 s. *Counter:* passive; nothing to dodge, play around what triggers it.
- **1 NeNe Kappa** *(ground circle)*. Damage: 80/120/160/200/240; Damage Scaling: 55% INT; Damage: 35/45/55/65/75; Range: 11.2 m; Radius: 0.8 m; Cooldown: 14/13.5/13/12.5/12 s; Cost: 60/65/70/75/80 mana. Mechanics: enemy CC: slow. *Counter:* Bouncing kappa: stay away from walls it can ricochet off; sidestep the throw.
- **2 Shell Spikes** *(self buff / empowered attack)*. Damage Reflected: 15/20/25/30/35% of Damage Taken; Cooldown Reduction: 0.6/0.7/0.8/0.9/1 s; Buff Duration: 3 s; Cooldown: 14/13.5/13/12.5/12 s; Cost: 50 mana. *Counter:* Reflects damage for 3 s: stop attacking him until it ends.
- **3 Sumo Slam** *(placed object/summon, dash)*. Damage: 60/95/130/165/200; Damage Scaling: 40% INT; Radius: 0.8 m; Cooldown: 14 s; Cost: 50/55/60/65/70 mana. *Counter:* Dash that silences and pushes the first god: sidestep it; stay away from walls (wall impact damages).
- **U Watery Grave** *(placed object/summon, circle around the god)*. First Hit Damage: 80/130/180/230/280; First Hit Damage Scaling: 40% INT; Subsequent Hits Damage: 40/65/90/115/140; Subsequent Hits Damage Scaling: 20% INT; Movement Speed: 30%; Damage Mitigation: 20%; Radius: 3.6 m; Cooldown: 75 s; Cost: 60/65/70/75/80 mana. Mechanics: enemy CC: knockback, pull. *Counter:* Spinning knock-backs around him: leave the area; he is CC immune.
- *Aspect version (numbers as listed under the page's God Aspect section):* P Water Bowl: Lifesteal: 3% Per Kappa | 2 Shell Spikes: Thrown Damage: 40/70/100/130/160; Thrown Damage Scaling: 50% INT; Orbit Damage: 10/25/40/55/70; Orbit Damage Scaling: 5% INT + 15% Protections; Range: 11.2 m; Cooldown: 8/7.5/7/6.5/6 s; Cost: 50 mana.

##### Loki
Source: [S2 wiki, Loki](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Loki) (revision of 2026-10-19; raw text via [wiki API](https://wiki.smite2.com/api.php?action=query&prop=revisions&rvprop=content&rvslots=main&format=json&titles=Loki)).
- **Basic attack:** Damage Scaling: 100% STR + 20% INT + 100% Attack Damage; Range: 1.92 m; Cone Angle: 120 deg.
- **P Behind You** *(passive)*. Damage: 115%. *Counter:* passive; nothing to dodge, play around what triggers it.
- **1 Vanish** *(stealth)*. Damage Per Tick: 30/45/60/75/90; Damage Scaling Per Tick: 20% STR; Movement Speed: 35%; Buff Duration: 4 s; Cooldown: 15 s; Cost: 60/65/70/75/80 mana. Mechanics: 4 hits over 2 s. *Counter (rule-based):* Damage partly reveals it and hard CC breaks it; sweep the area where it vanished.
- **2 Agonizing Visions** *(placed object/summon)*. Damage: 20/25/30/35/40; Damage Scaling: 15% STR; Blind Duration: 3 s; Damage Dealt Reduction: 5%; Debuff Duration: 2.5 s; Max Debuff Stacks: 3; Range: 8.8 m; Radius: 4 m; Cooldown: 13 s; Cost: 50/55/60/65/70 mana. Mechanics: 8 hits over 3.5 s; enemy CC: blind. *Counter:* The vision blocks enemy movement like a wall and blinds on the fourth hit: leave its 4 m area within a few hits.
- **3 Flurry Strike** *(cone 90 deg, channel)*. Damage: 20/30/40/50/60; Damage Scaling: 25% STR; Final Hit Damage: 55/75/95/115/135; Final Hit Damage Scaling: 60% STR; Slow: 15%; Final Hit Slow: 30%; Slow Duration: 2.25 s; Radius: 3.2 m; Cone Angle: 90 deg; Cooldown: 9 s; Cost: 40/50/60/70/80 mana. Mechanics: 6 hits over 1.5 s; caster displacement immune; enemy CC: slow. *Counter:* Channelled cone: step behind or beside him, or hit him with hard CC (not displacement).
- **U Assassinate** *(teleport)*. First Strike Damage: 70/105/140/175/210; First Strike Damage Scaling: 35% STR; Second Strike Damage: 100/145/190/235/280; Second Strike Damage Scaling: 80% STR; Stun Duration: 0.75 s; Teleport Range: 8.8 m; First Strike Radius: 1.6 m; Second Strike Range: 4.8 m; Second Strike Cone Angle: 105 deg; Cooldown: 90 s; Cost: 90 mana. Mechanics: enemy CC: stun. *Counter:* He teleports to the god nearest the cursor: cripple stops it; Beads cleanse the stun.
- *Aspect version (numbers as listed under the page's God Aspect section):* P Behind You: Damage Scaling: 2% Max Health + 10% Protections | 1 Vanish: Movement Speed: 35%; Buff Duration: 4 s; Blind Duration: 3 s; Cooldown: 15/14.5/14/13.5/13 s; Cost: 60/65/70/75/80 mana | 2 Agonizing Visions: Damage: 20/25/30/35/40; Damage Scaling: 15% STR; Damage Mitigation: 15/17.5/20/22.5/25%; Damage Dealt Reduction: 5%; Blind Duration: 3 s; Debuff Duration: 2.5 s; Max Debuff Stacks: 3; Slow: 15%; Range: 8.8 m; Radius: 4 m; Cooldown: 13 s; Cost: 50/55/60/65/70 mana.

##### Medusa
Source: [S2 wiki, Medusa](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Medusa) (revision of 2026-10-10; raw text via [wiki API](https://wiki.smite2.com/api.php?action=query&prop=revisions&rvprop=content&rvslots=main&format=json&titles=Medusa)).
- **Basic attack:** Damage Scaling: 100% STR + 20% INT + 100% Attack Damage; Range: 8.8 m; Radius: 0.48 m.
- **P Sidewinder** *(passive)*. no numbers listed on the wiki. *Counter:* passive; nothing to dodge, play around what triggers it.
- **1 Viper Shot** *(ground circle)*. Damage Per Tick: 10/15/20/25/30; Damage Scaling Per Tick: 8% STR + 8% INT; Attack Speed: 40/45/50/55/60%; Attack Speed Scaling: 15% STR; Range: 8.8 m; Radius: 0.48 m; Cooldown: 12/11.5/11/10.5/10 s; Cost: 50 mana. *Counter:* Empowered basics: dodge her basic attacks, or disengage for the buff.
- **2 Acid Spray** *(projectile, cone 70 deg)*. Damage: 90/145/200/255/310; Damage Scaling: 65% STR + 80% INT; Healing Reduction: -40%; Healing Reduction Duration: 5 s; Range: 7.2 m; Radius: 0.64 m; Cone Range: 7.2 m; Cone Angle: 70 deg; Cooldown: 14/13.5/13/12.5/12 s; Cost: 40/45/50/55/60 mana. Mechanics: projectile, stops on the first unit (minion or god), walls block it. *Counter:* Hits the first enemy then sprays a cone behind it: do not stand behind a teammate she is shooting.
- **3 Lacerate** *(projectile, dash)*. Damage: 80/130/180/230/280; Damage Scaling: 75% STR + 40% INT; Root Duration: 1 s; Range: 8.8 m; Radius: 0.96 m; Cooldown: 16/15.5/15/14.5/14 s; Cost: 70 mana. Mechanics: passes minions, stops on the first god, walls block it; enemy CC: root. *Counter:* A dash that roots at the end: cripple denies it; Beads cleanse the root.
- **U Petrify** *(piercing line, cone 40 deg)*. Damage: 270/365/460/555/650; Damage Scaling: 80% STR + 95% INT; Not Looking Damage Reduction: -15%; Slow: 20/25/30/35/40%; Slow Duration: 3 s; Stun Duration: 2 s; Temporary STR and INT Buff: 10%; Buff Duration: 10 s; Permanent STR and INT Buff: 5; Range: 11.2 m; Cone Angle: 40 deg; Cooldown: 90 s; Cost: 100 mana. Mechanics: passes through all enemies, stopped by walls; caster CC immune; enemy CC: stun, slow. *Counter:* Stuns only if you face her: turn your back as she casts.
- *Aspect:* none listed on the page.

##### Mercury
Source: [S2 wiki, Mercury](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Mercury) (revision of 2026-10-11; raw text via [wiki API](https://wiki.smite2.com/api.php?action=query&prop=revisions&rvprop=content&rvslots=main&format=json&titles=Mercury)).
- **Basic attack:** Damage Scaling: 100% STR + 20% INT + 100% Attack Damage; Range: 1.92 m; Cone Angle: 120 deg.
- **P Fastest God Alive** *(passive)*. Max Stacks: 500; Max Damage Boost: 25%. *Counter:* passive; nothing to dodge, play around what triggers it.
- **1 Made You Look** *(ground circle)*. Damage: 80/115/150/185/220; Damage Scaling: 70% Basic Attack Damage; Range: 7.68 m; Radius: 1.4/1.6/1.8/2/2.2 m; Cooldown: 11/10.5/10/9.5/9 s; Cost: 50 mana. *Counter:* Instant area at the cursor: keep juking.
- **2 Maximum Velocity** *(self buff / empowered attack)*. Attack Speed: 30/40/50/60/70%; Buff Duration: 5 s; Movement Speed: 2/4/6/8/10%; Cooldown: 14/13.5/13/12.5/12 s; Cost: 60/65/70/75/80 mana; no damage values listed. *Counter (rule-based):* Self buff: back off and wait out its 5 s window, then trade.
- **3 Special Delivery** *(projectile, dash)*. Damage: 15/20/25/30/35; Damage Scaling: 20% STR; Minion Damage: 70/115/160/205/250; Minion Damage Scaling: 64% STR; Stun Duration: 1 s; Range: 8.8 m; Radius: 0.48 m; Cooldown: 15 s; Cost: 60/65/70/75/80 mana. Mechanics: passes minions, stops on the first god, walls block it; enemy CC: stun, grab. *Counter:* Dash grab that throws: sidestep it or keep a god in front.
- **U Sonic Boom** *(dash)*. Damage: 200/275/350/425/500; Damage Scaling: 100% STR; Stun Duration: 1.25 s; Minimum Speed Buff: 15%; Maximum Speed Buff: 30%; Buff Duration: 5 s; Min Range: 0 m; Max Range: 48 m; Radius: 2.59 m; Cooldown: 90/85/80/75/70 s; Cost: 80/85/90/95/100 mana. Mechanics: caster CC immune; enemy CC: stun. *Counter:* Long charged dash: watch for the charge-up and step out of its line.
- *Aspect:* none listed on the page.

##### Merlin
Source: [S2 wiki, Merlin](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Merlin) (revision of 2026-10-20; raw text via [wiki API](https://wiki.smite2.com/api.php?action=query&prop=revisions&rvprop=content&rvslots=main&format=json&titles=Merlin)).
- **Basic attack:** Damage Scaling: 100% STR + 20% INT + 100% Attack Damage; Range: 8.8 m; Radius: 0.48 m.
- **P Overload** *(passive)*. Damage: 20; Damage Scaling: 20% INT; Max Stacks: 3; Buff Duration: 5 s. *Counter:* passive; nothing to dodge, play around what triggers it.
- **1 Eclipse** (Arcane stance) *(piercing line)*. Damage: 50/75/100/125/150; Damage Scaling: 55% INT; Damage Per Tick: 5/15/25/35/45; Damage Scaling Per Tick: 10% INT; Range: 9.6 m; Initial Radius: 0.8 m; Max Radius: 2.4 m; Outer Radius: 4 m; Cooldown: 12 s; Cost: 60 mana. Mechanics: passes through all enemies and walls (cannot be blocked). *Counter (rule-based):* Unblockable: strafe out of its width (4 m) or stay beyond 9.6 m.
- **2 Vortex** (Arcane stance) *(placed object/summon, delay (length not listed))*. Damage: 65/95/125/155/185; Damage Scaling: 70% INT; Range: 9.6 m; Radius: 3.2 m; Cooldown: 14/13.5/13/12.5/12 s; Cost: 60/65/70/75/80 mana. Mechanics: enemy CC: pull, vortex. *Counter:* Leave the field before the delay ends, or you are pulled to its centre.
- **3 Flicker** (Arcane stance) *(teleport)*. Heal: 22.5% of Recent Damage Taken; Range: 4.8 m; Cooldown: 24/23/22/21/20 s; Cost: 70 mana; no damage values listed. *Counter:* Short teleport: cripple prevents it.
- **U Elemental Mastery** (Arcane stance) *(circle around the god)*. Damage: 0/90/120/150/180/220; Damage Scaling: 25% INT; Radius: 4 m; Cooldown: 40/40/35/30/25/20 s; Cost: 40/40/50/60/70/80 mana. *Counter (rule-based):* Keep more than 4 m from the god while it is active.
- **1 Radiate** (Fire stance) *(channel, ground circle)*. Damage: 12/19/26/33/40; Damage Scaling: 20% INT; Damage Per Tick: 5/10/15/20/25; Damage Scaling Per Tick: 5% INT; Range: 7.2 m; Radius: 0.8 m; Cooldown: 14/13.5/13/12.5/12 s; Cost: 60/65/70/75/80 mana. *Counter:* Channelled beam: strafe out, or interrupt him.
- **2 Dragonfire** (Fire stance) *(placed object/summon, cone 80 deg)*. Damage: 12/15/18/21/24; Damage Scaling: 20% INT; Protections Reduced: 4% Per Stack; Max Debuff Stacks: 4; Deploy Range: 8 m; Range: 4 m; Cone Angle: 80 deg; Cooldown: 16 s; Cost: 60/65/70/75/80 mana. *Counter (rule-based):* Placed object or summon: stay out of its reach, or destroy or outwait it. Stay outside the 80 deg cone (4 m), or get beside or behind the god.
- **3 Flicker** (Fire stance) *(teleport)*. Range: 4.8 m; Cooldown: 24/23/22/21/20 s; Cost: 70 mana; no damage values listed. *Counter:* Short teleport: cripple prevents it.
- **U Elemental Mastery** (Fire stance) *(circle around the god)*. Damage: 0/90/120/150/180/220; Damage Scaling: 25% INT; Radius: 4 m; Cooldown: 40/40/35/30/25/20 s; Cost: 40/40/50/60/70/80 mana. *Counter (rule-based):* Keep more than 4 m from the god while it is active.
- **1 Frostbolt** (Ice stance) *(projectile)*. Damage: 60/95/130/165/200; Damage Scaling: 55% INT; Range: 11.2 m; Radius: 0.64 m; Explosion Radius: 3.2 m; Cooldown: 6 s; Cost: 60 mana. Mechanics: projectile, stops on the first unit (minion or god), walls block it. *Counter (rule-based):* Sidestep it, or keep a minion or wall between you: it stops on the first unit.
- **2 Blizzard** (Ice stance) *(ground circle)*. Damage Per Tick: 10/25/40/55/70; Damage Scaling Per Tick: 15% INT; Slow: 8% Per Stack; Max Debuff Stacks: 5; Slow Duration: 2 s; Range: 8.8 m; Radius: 3.2 m; Cooldown: 6 s; Cost: 60 mana. Mechanics: 8 hits over 4 s; enemy CC: slow. *Counter:* Leave the blizzard; it slows the longer you stay.
- **3 Flicker** (Ice stance) *(teleport)*. Cooldown Reduction: 2 s; Range: 4.8 m; Cooldown: 24/23/22/21/20 s; Cost: 70 mana; no damage values listed. *Counter:* Short teleport: cripple prevents it.
- **U Elemental Mastery** (Ice stance) *(circle around the god)*. Damage: 0/90/120/150/180/220; Damage Scaling: 25% INT; Slow: 0/20/20/20/20/20%; Slow Duration: 0/2/2/2/2/2 s; Radius: 4 m; Cooldown: 40/40/35/30/25/20 s; Cost: 40/40/50/60/70/80 mana. Mechanics: enemy CC: slow. *Counter (rule-based):* Keep more than 4 m from the god while it is active.
- *Aspect version (numbers as listed under the page's God Aspect section):* U Elemental Mastery: Damage: 0/90/120/150/180/220; Damage Scaling: 25% INT; INT: 10; INT Scaling: 0.5 Per Level; Buff Duration: 10 s; Slow: 0/20/20/20/20/20%; Slow Duration: 0/2/2/2/2/2 s; Radius: 4 m; Cooldown: 20 s; Cost: 40/40/50/60/70/80 mana.

##### Mordred

- **2 Bloodrage** *(circle around the god)*. Damage: 70/115/160/205/250; Damage Scaling: 60% STR + 40% INT; Attack Speed: 32/34/36/38/40%; Bonus Attack Speed: 5% Per Stack; Movement Speed: 12/14/16/18/20%; Protections: 6/7/8/9/10 Per Stack; Buff Duration: 4/4.5/5/5.5/6 s; Protections Reduced: -4/-5/-6/-7/-8; Debuff Duration: 4/4.5/5/5.5/6 s; Radius: 3.2 m; Cooldown: 14 s; Cost: 50/55/60/65/70 mana. *Counter (rule-based):* Keep more than 3.2 m from the god while it is active.
- **3 Severing Slice** *(piercing line)*. Damage: 55/85/115/145/175; Damage Scaling: 70% INT; Bleed Damage: 25/30/35/40/45; Bleed Damage Scaling: 12.5% STR; Minion Damage: 15/20/25/30/35; Minion Damage Scaling: 5% STR; Slow: 30%; Debuff Duration: 2.5 s; Range: 9.6 m; Radius: 0.6 m; Inner Radius: 0.2 m; Cooldown: 12 s; Cost: 40/50/60/70/80 mana. - **U Heart Slash** *(piercing line)*. Damage: 100/155/210/265/320; Damage Scaling: 75% STR; Damage Per Tick: 30/40/50/60/70; Damage Scaling Per Tick: 25% INT; Heal: 8/16/24/32/40; Heal Scaling: 12.5% INT; Shield Health: 160/200/240/280/320; Range: 6 m; Radius: 1 m; Cooldown: 20 s; Cost: 40 Wrath. - **1 Universe Ring Toss** *(projectile, aimed line of strikes (wiki lists Radius > Range))*. Initial Damage: 90/120/150/180/210; Initial Damage Scaling: 60% STR; Bounce Damage: 75/100/125/150/175; Bounce Damage Scaling: 30% STR; Free Ring Damage: 80/110/140/170/200; Free Ring Damage Scaling: 90% STR; Physical Protection Reduced: 5/7/9/11/13%; Debuff Duration: 1 s; Slow: 12.5%; Slow Duration: 1 s; Debuff Duration: 5%; Movement Speed: 5%; Buff Duration: 2 s; Bounces: 4/5/6/7/8; Range: 0.96 m; Radius: 8.8 m; Cooldown: 10 s; Cost: 50/55/60/65/70 mana. Mechanics: projectile, stops on the first unit (minion or god), walls block it; enemy CC: slow. **Data flag:** the wiki lists Range 0.96 m and Radius 8.8 m; the two labels look swapped (unconfirmed). *Counter:* Bouncing ring: spread out so it cannot chain.
- **2 Flaming Spear** *(self buff / empowered attack)*. Attack Speed: 35/40/45/50/55%; Critical Strike Chance: 20%; Buff Duration: 4/4.5/5/5.5/6 s; Heal: 4/6/8/10/12 Per Stack; Cooldown: 14 s; Cost: 55/60/65/70/75 mana; no damage values listed. *Counter (rule-based):* Self buff: back off and wait out its 4 s window, then trade.

- **U Wind Fire Wheels** *(dash)*. Initial Damage: 50/105/160/215/270; Initial Damage Scaling: 65% STR; Combo Damage: 25/45/65/85/105; Combo Damage Scaling: 55% STR; Landing Damage: 50/105/160/215/270; Landing Damage Scaling: 65% STR; Protections Buff: 30/35/40/45/50; Protections Buff Duration: 5 s; Range: 8.8 m; Radius: 0.96 m; Cooldown: 90 s; Cost: 50/55/60/65/70 mana. *Counter:* Dash that launches the first god: sidestep it; he is CC immune during it.

- **1 Verdant Growth** *(ground circle)*. - **U Wrath of Terra** *(circle around the god)*. Damage: 60/75/90/105/120; Damage Scaling: 25% INT; Radius: 5.6 m; Cooldown: 90 s; Cost: 100 mana. Mechanics: enemy CC: knockup. *Counter:* Knock-up around him: stay out of reach.

##### Thanatos
Source: [S2 wiki, Thanatos](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Thanatos) (revision of 2026-10-07; raw text via [wiki API](https://wiki.smite2.com/api.php?action=query&prop=revisions&rvprop=content&rvslots=main&format=json&titles=Thanatos)).
- **Basic attack:** Damage Scaling: 100% STR + 20% INT + 100% Attack Damage; Range: 1.92 m; Cone Angle: 120 deg.
- **P Harvester of Souls** *(passive)*. God Kill Heal: 15% of their Max Health; NPC Kill Heal: 10% of their Max Health; Cooldown Reduction: -5 s. *Counter:* passive; nothing to dodge, play around what triggers it.
- **1 Death Scythe** *(projectile)*. Damage: 95/165/235/305/375; Damage Scaling: 85% STR; Slow: 20%; Range: 11.2 m; Radius: 0.48 m; Cooldown: 11 s; Cost: 4% Health. Mechanics: projectile, stops on the first unit (minion or god), walls block it; enemy CC: slow. *Counter:* Sidestep the scythe.
- **2 Scent of Death** *(self buff / empowered attack)*. Bonus Damage: 15%; Movement Speed: 8/12/16/20/24%; Bonus Movement Speed: Up to 8/12/16/20/24%; Penetration: 8/12/16/20/24; Buff Duration: 6 s; Cooldown: 13 s; Cost: 4% Health. *Counter:* He gains speed: kite him.
- **3 Soul Reap** *(cone 110 deg)*. Damage: 100/150/200/250/300; Damage Scaling: 80% STR; Silence Duration: 1 s; Radius: 5.6 m; Cone Angle: 110 deg; Cooldown: 14/13.5/13/12.5/12 s; Cost: 4% Health. Mechanics: enemy CC: silence. *Counter:* Frontal cone that silences: stay beside him; Beads cleanse the silence.
- **U Hovering Death** *(leap)*. Damage: 110/145/180/215/250; Damage Scaling: 80% STR; Execute Health %: 24/28/32/36/40%; Stun Duration: 1 s; Radius: 2.4 m; Cooldown: 90 s; Cost: 6% Health. Mechanics: caster CC immune; enemy CC: stun. *Counter:* Flight then a dive that stuns and executes: move away from his shadow and stay above the threshold.

- *Aspect:* none listed on the page.

### Inferences
- The counter lines say the same few things again and again, because Smite 2 has few kinds of counterplay: sidestep a shot, hide behind a unit that stops it, leave a circle before its delay, stand beside a cone, cripple a dash or leap before it starts, interrupt a channel, cleanse hard CC with Beads, and wait out a buff or immunity. A game borrowing these kits can show each of them on the targeter.
- Damage and cost grow linearly with rank on almost every ability, so a rank-1 and a rank-5 value describe the whole series.

### Gaps
- **Second source.** Mobalytics and other Smite 2 sites were not fetched, so no number here is confirmed by a second source.
- **Delays and travel speeds.** Only 16 abilities mention a delay or wind-up in their wiki text, and only 5 give its length (Chronos' Stop Time 1 s, Cupid's Share the Love 0.75 s, Nut's Skyfall 1 s, Ra's Divine Light 2 s, Ymir's Shards of Ice 3 s); the other 11 are marked "delay (length not listed)". Ground circles without such a mention show "no listed delay", even where the game may have a short wind-up. Projectile speeds are not on the wiki pages.
- **Counter lines.** The rule-based ones follow the wiki's stop rules and shapes but were not checked in play. Some forms are approximate where the wiki's stats are ambiguous (for example "ground circle" for an ability that is really a lobbed projectile).
- **Aspect numbers** are copied from the page's "God Aspect" section; for aspects the page sometimes lists only the changed stats, so missing aspect values mean "same as the base ability" or "not listed".

## What kind of ability is each one, and what counters each kind?

### Takeaway
Across the 96 gods, ground circles and circles around the god are the most common forms, then projectiles and piercing lines, then dashes, leaps and cones. The wiki writes one of a few standard stop rules on each shot, and that rule decides the counterplay: a shot that stops on the first unit can be blocked by a minion, one that only stops on gods can be blocked only by another god, and one that passes everything can only be strafed.

### Cited Findings
- **Forms of the active abilities and passives** (base kits only, all 96 gods; counted by script from the wiki stats as described at the top; one ability can have several forms) — from the per-god pages linked above, e.g. [S2 wiki, Loki](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Loki):

| Form | Count |
|---|---|
| passive | 98 |
| ground circle | 68 |
| piercing line | 58 |
| dash | 52 |
| circle around the god | 50 |
| projectile | 42 |
| placed object/summon | 42 |
| leap | 42 |
| cone | 41 |
| self buff / empowered attack | 38 |
| channel | 35 |
| delay | 16 |
| teleport | 14 |
| stealth | 8 |
| area, walls stop it | 4 |
| aimed line of strikes (wiki lists Radius > Range) | 4 |
| aimed ability | 2 |

- **Stop rules as the wiki words them** (counted over base-kit abilities whose notes use one of the standard sentences; paraphrased) — per-god pages, e.g. [S2 wiki, Anubis](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Anubis):

| What the ability stops on | Abilities |
|---|---|
| passes through all enemies and walls (cannot be blocked) | 46 |
| passes minions, stops on the first god, walls block it | 19 |
| projectile, stops on the first unit (minion or god), walls block it | 15 |
| passes through all enemies, stopped by walls | 12 |
| passes minions and walls, stops on the first god | 8 |
| hits everything in its area, walls stop it | 4 |

- **Cleansing.** Every Smite 2 player has Purification Beads; abilities that say "CC immune while active" ignore enemy CC, and "displacement immune" ignores only knock-backs, pulls and knock-ups ([S2 wiki, Crowd Control](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Crowd_Control); smite-gods.md header). Cripple stops dashes, leaps and teleports ([S2 wiki, Crowd Control](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Crowd_Control)).
- **Stealth.** Loki's page states that taking damage partly reveals a stealthed god for 0.165 s, that stealth breaks on hard CC, and that structures reveal it ([S2 wiki, Loki](https://wiki.smite2.com/w/Loki)).

### Inferences
- **Counter by form** (the rules the counter lines use):
  - *Projectile that stops on the first unit:* sidestep, or keep a minion or wall in front.
  - *Passes minions, stops on gods:* only another god (or a wall, if walls stop it) blocks it.
  - *Passes everything, including walls:* only strafing or range works.
  - *Ground circle:* juke while it is cast; if the wiki lists a delay, step out before it ends.
  - *Circle around the god / cone:* spacing; stand beside or behind a cone.
  - *Dash:* cripple before it starts; a dash stops on walls and gods, so body-block it.
  - *Leap:* cripple before it starts; it passes over units, so punish the landing spot.
  - *Teleport:* cripple prevents it.
  - *Channel:* hard CC interrupts it unless the caster is CC immune ("displacement immune" channels still break to stuns and silences); otherwise leave the area.
  - *Stealth:* damage reveals briefly and hard CC breaks it.
  - *Self buff, shield, immunity, reflect:* back off and wait out the duration.
  - *Hard CC of any kind:* Beads cleanse it, so the attacker holds CC until Beads are spent.
- For a top-down 1v1 game without minions, "stops on the first unit" and "stops on gods" shots both become plain skillshots, so the delay and the width carry the counterplay.

### Gaps
- The tallies count the forms the script derived; hand-checking was done on the counter lines, not on every form tag.
